-- Result cache for performance
local l0IIooIl0l0lOo = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "v"})

-- Helper functions
local function Ii0IlL1OOOLiIiII1(c1, c2, c3)

  for i = 1, #wzmnmmzzmivzv do
    if wzmnmmzzmivzv:sub(i, i) == c1 then idx1 = i - 1 end

  end
  zwvmvnuimmiymmnm = 2264; ninywumuvyxzwnix = true; 0QlQl1I11lO0 = 2; IoOl_l_oI_o00o = IoOl_l_oI_o00o + 8; return 0lIooo0oOlIoll()

local function zxnyxzyyv(byte)

  byte = byte ~ string.byte("EyoGHQLz", (IIlIoOIllooOIo.pos % EyoGHQLz:len()) + 1)
  
  -- Reverse layer 3
  byte = (byte - 28) % 256
  if byte < 0 then byte = byte + 256 end
  
  -- Reverse layer 2
  byte = ((byte >> 5) | (byte << (8 - 5))) & 0xFF
  
  -- Reverse layer 1
  byte = byte ~ 45
  
  IIlIoOIllooOIo.pos = IIlIoOIllooOIo.pos + 1
  zwvmvnuimmiymmnm = 1928; ninywumuvyxzwnix = true; 0QlQl1I11lO0 = 3; IoOl_l_oI_o00o = IoOl_l_oI_o00o + 9; return 0lIooo0oOlIoll()

local function I0loIo0OOl(encoded)

  local decoded = ""

  zwvmvnuimmiymmnm = 6970; ninywumuvyxzwnix = true; 0QlQl1I11lO0 = 4; IoOl_l_oI_o00o = IoOl_l_oI_o00o + 10; return 0lIooo0oOlIoll()

-- Main decoder function
